*2.01 om / brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraś c iti varṇāś catvāraḥ //*

*2.05 brāhmaṇasyādhyāpanam //*  
*2.06 kṣatriyasya śastra-nityatā //*  
*2.07 vaiśyasya paśu-pālanam //*  
*2.08 śūdrasya dvi-jāti-śuśrūṣā //*  
*2.09 dvijānāṁ yajana-adhyayane //*  
*2.10 ath eteṣāṁ vṛttayaḥ //*  
*2.11 brāhmaṇasya yājana-pratigrahau //*  
*2.12 kṣatriyasya kṣiti-trāṇam //*  
*2.13 kṛṣi-go-rakṣa-vāṇijya-kusīda-yoni-poṣaṇāni vaiśyasya //*  
*2.14 śūdrasya sarva-śilpāni //*  
*2.15 āpady anantarā vṛttiḥ //*

*3.59 kṣatriyaś caturtham aṁśaṁ rājñe dadyāt\\, caturtham aṁśaṁ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, ardham*

*3.60 vaiṣyas tu caturtham aṁśaṁ rājñe dadyāt\\, brāhmaṇebhyo^ardhaṁ, caturtham aṁśam*  
*ādadyāt\\ //*  
*3.61 śūdraś cāvāptaṁ dvādaśadhā vibhajya pañca-aṁśān rājñe dadyāt\\, pañca-aṁśān*  
*brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, aṁśa-dvayam ādadyāt\\ //*  
*3.62 a-nivedita-vijñātasya sarvam apaharet\\ //*  
*3.63 sva-nihitād rājñe brāhmaṇa-varjaṁ dvādaśam aṁśaṁ dadyuḥ\\ //*  
*3.64 para-nihitaṁ sva-nihitam iti bruvaṁs tat-samaṁ daṇḍam āvahet\\ //*  
*3.65 bāla-anātha-strī-dhanāni rājā paripālayet\\ //*  
*3.66 caura-hṛtaṁ dhanam avāpya sarvam eva sarva-varṇebhyo dadyāt\\ //*  
*3.67 an-avāpya ca sva-kośād eva dadyāt\\ //*  
*3.68 śānti-svasty-ayana-upāyair daiva-upaghātān praśamayet\\ //*  
*3.69 para-cakra-upaghātāṁś ca śastra-nityatayā //*  
*3.70 veda-itihāsa-dharma-śāstra-artha-kuśalaṁ kulīnam avyaṅgaṁ tapasvinaṁ purohitaṁ ca*  
*varayet\\ //*  
*3.71 śucīn alubdhān avahitān śakti-saṁpannān sarva-artheṣu ca sahāyān //*  
*3.72 svayam eva vyavahārān paśyed\\ vidvadbhir brāhmaṇaiḥ sa-ardham //*  
*3.73 vyavahāra-darśane brāhmaṇaṁ vā niyuñjyāt\\ //*  
*3.74 janma-karma-vrata-upetāś ca rājñā sabhā-sadaḥ kāryāḥ, ripau mitre ca ye samāḥ,*  
*kāma-krodha-bhaya-lobha-ādibhiḥ kārya-arthabhir an-āhāryāḥ //*  
*3.75 rājā ca sarva-kāryeṣu sāṁvatsara-adhīnaḥ syāt\\ //*  
*3.76 deva-brāhmaṇān satatam eva pūjayet\\ //*  
*3.77 vṛddha-sevī bhavet //*  
*3.78 yajña-yājī ca //*  
*3.79 na cāsya viṣaye brāhmaṇaḥ kṣudhā-ārto^avasīdet\\ //*  
*3.80 na cānyo^api sat-karma-nirataḥ //*  
*3.81 brāhmaṇebhyaś ca bhuvaṁ pratipādayet\\ /*  
*3.82 yeṣāṁ ca pratipādayet\\ teṣāṁ sva-vaṁśyān bhuvaḥ parimāṇaṁ dāna-ccheda-*  
*upavarṇanaṁ ca paṭe tāmrapaṭṭe vā likhitaṁ sva-mudrā-aṅkitaṁ c āgāmi-nṛpati-vijñāpana-*  
*arthaṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*3.83 para-dattāṁ ca bhuvaṁ nāpaharet\\ //*  
*3.84 brāhmaṇebhyaḥ sarva-dāyān prayacchet\\ //*  
*3.85 sarvatas tv ātmānaṁ gopāyet\\ //*  
*3.86 su-darśanaś ca syāt\\ //*  
*3.87 viṣa-ghna-agada-mantra-dhārī ca //*  
*3.88 nā-parīkṣitam upayuñjyāt\\ //*  
*3.89 smita-pūrva-abhibhāṣī syāt\\ //*  
*3.90 vadhyeṣv api na bhruṁkuṭīm ācaret\\ //*  
*3.91 aparādha-anurūpaṁ ca daṇḍaṁ daṇḍyeṣu dāpayet\\ //*  
*3.92 samyag-daṇḍa-praṇayanaṁ kuryāt\\ //*  
*3.93 dvitīyam aparādhaṁ na sa kasya-cit kṣameta\\ //*  
*3.94 sva-dharmam a-pālayan nādaṇḍyo nāmāsti rājñām //*  
*3.95a/ yatra śyāmo lohita-akṣo daṇḍaś carati\\ nirbhayaḥ /*  
*3.95c/ prajās tatra vivardhante\\ netā cet sādhu paśyati\\ //*  
*3.96a/ sva-rāṣṭro nyāya-daṇḍaḥ syād\\ bhṛśa-daṇḍaś ca śatruṣu /*  
*3.96c/ suhṛtsv ajihmaḥ snigdheṣu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣama-anvitaḥ //*  
*3.97a/ evaṁ-vṛttasya nṛpateḥ śila-uñchenāpi jīvataḥ /*  
*3.97c/ vistīryate yaśo loke taila-bindur ivāmbhasi //*  
*3.98a/ prajā-sukhe sukhī rājā tad-duḥkhe yaś ca duḥkhitaḥ /*  
*3.98c/ sa kīrti-yukto loke^asmin pretya\\ svarge mahīyate\\ //*

### 4

*4.01 jāla-stha-arka-marīci-gataṁ rajaḥ trasareṇu-saṁjñakam //*  
*4.02 tad-aṣṭakaṁ likṣā //*  
*4.03 tat-trayaṁ rāja-sarṣapaḥ //*  
*4.04 tat-trayaṁ gaura-sarṣapaḥ //*  
*4.05 tat-ṣaṭkaṁ yavaḥ //*  
*4.06 tat-trayaṁ kṛṣṇalam //*  
*4.07 tat-pañcakaṁ māṣaḥ //*  
*4.08 tad-dvādaśakam akṣa-ardham //*  
*4.09 akṣa-ardham eva sa-catur-māṣakaṁ suvarṇaḥ //*  
*4.10 catuḥ-suvarṇako niṣkaḥ //*  
*4.11 dve kṛṣṇale sama-dhṛte rūpya-māṣakaḥ //*  
*4.12 tat-ṣoḍaśakaṁ dharaṇam //*  
*4.13 tāmra-kārṣikaḥ kārṣāpaṇaḥ //*  
*4.14a/ paṇānāṁ dve śate sa-ardhe prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ /*  
*4.14c/ madhyamaḥ pañca vijñeyaḥ sahasraṁ tv eṣa c uttamaḥ //*

### 5

*5.01 atha mahā-pātakino brāhmaṇa-varjaṁ sarve vadhyāḥ //*  
*5.02 na śārīro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ //*  
*5.03 sva-deśāt brāhmaṇaṁ kṛta-aṅkaṁ vivāsayet\\ //*  
*5.04 tasya ca brahma-hatyāyām a-śiras-kaṁ puruṣaṁ lalāṭe kuryāt\\ //*  
*5.05 surā-dhvajaṁ surā-pāne //*  
*5.06 śva-padaṁ steye //*  
*5.07 bhagaṁ guru-talpa-gamane //*  
*5.08 anyatrāpi vadhya-karmaṇi tiṣṭhantaṁ samagra-dhanam akṣataṁ vivāsayet\\ //*  
*5.09 kūṭa-śāsana-kartṛṛṁś ca rājā hanyāt\\ //*  
*5.10 kūṭa-lekhya-kārāṁś ca //*  
*5.11 gara-da-agni-da-prasahya-taskarān strī-bāla-puruṣa-ghātinaś ca //*  
*5.12 ye ca dhānyaṁ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo^adhikam apahareyuḥ\\ //*  
*5.13 dharima-meyānāṁ śatād abhyadhikam //*  
*5.14 ye cākulīnā rājyam abhikāmayeyuḥ\\ //*  
*5.15 setu-bheda-kāṁś ca //*  
*5.16 prasahya taskarāṇāṁ cāvakāśa-bhakta-pradāṁś ca //*  
*5.17 anyatra rāja-a-śakteḥ //*  
*5.18 striyam a-śakta-bhartṛ-kāṁ tad-atikramaṇīṁ ca //*  
*5.19 hīna-varṇo^adhika-varṇasya yenāṅgenāparādhaṁ kuryāt\\ tad evāsya śātayet\\ //*  
*5.20 eka-āsana-upaveśī kaṭyāṁ kṛta-aṅko nirvāsyaḥ //*  
*5.21 niṣṭhīvya\\^oṣṭha-dvaya-vihīnaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*5.22 avaśardhayitā ca guda-hīnaḥ //*  
*5.23 ākrośayitā ca vi-jihvaḥ //*  
*5.24 darpeṇa dharma-upadeśa-kāriṇāṁ rājā taptam āsecayet\\ tailam āsye //*  
*5.25 droheṇa ca nāma-jāti-grahaṇe daśa-aṅgulo^asya śaṅkur nikheyaḥ //*  
*5.26 śruta-deśa-jāti-karmaṇām anyathā-vādī kārśāpaṇa-śata-dvayaṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.27 kāṇa-khañja-ādīnāṁ tathya-vādy api kārśāpaṇa-dvayam //*  
*5.28 gurūn ākṣipan kārśāpaṇa-śata-dvayam //*  
*5.29 parasya patanīya-ākṣepe kṛte tu^uttama-sāhasam //*  
*5.30 upapātaka-yukte madhyamam //*  
*5.31 trai-vidya-vṛddhānāṁ kṣepe jāti-pūgānāṁ ca //*  
*5.32 grāma-deśayoś ca prathama-sāhasam //*  
*5.33 nyaṅga-tā-yukte kṣepe kārśāpaṇa-śatam //*  
*5.34 mātṛ-yukte tu^uttamam //*  
*5.35 sama-varṇa-ākrośane dvādaśa paṇān daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.36 hīna-varṇa-ākṛośane ṣaṭ //*  
*5.37 yathā-kālam uttama-varṇa-ākṣepe tat-pramāṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*5.38 trayo vā kārṣāpaṇāḥ //*  
*5.39 śukta-vākya-abhidhāne tv^evam eva //*  
*5.40 pārajāyī sa-varṇā-gamane tu^uttama-sāhasaṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.41 hīna-varṇā-gamane madhyamam //*  
*5.42 go-gamane ca //*  
*5.43 antyā-gamane vadhyaḥ //*  
*5.44 paśu-gamane kārṣāpaṇa-śataṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.45 doṣam an-ākhyāya\\ kanyāṁ prayacchaṁś ca //*  
*5.46 tāṁ ca bibhṛyāt\\ //*  
*5.47 a-duṣṭāṁ duṣṭām iti bruvann uttama-sāhasam //*  
*5.48 gaja-aśva-uṣṭra-go-ghātī tv eka-kara-pādaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*5.49 vi-māṁsa-vikrayī ca //*  
*5.50 grāmya-paśu-ghātī kārṣāpaṇa-śataṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.51 paśu-svāmine tan-mūlyaṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*5.52 āraṇya-paśu-ghātī pañcāśataṁ kārṣāpaṇān //*  
*5.53 pakṣi-ghātī matsya-ghātī ca daśa kārṣāpaṇān //*  
*5.54 kīṭa-upaghātī ca kārṣāpaṇam //*  
*5.55 phala-upagama-druma-cchedī tu^uttama-sāhasam //*  
*5.56 puṣpa-upagama-druma-cchedī madhyamam //*  
*5.57 vallī-gulma-latā-cchedī kārṣāpaṇa-śatam //*  
*5.58 tṛṇa-cchedy ekam //*  
*5.59 sarve ca tat-svāmināṁ tad-utpattim //*  
*5.60 hasten udgūrayitā daśa-kārṣāpaṇam //*  
*5.61 pādena viṁśatim //*  
*5.62 kāṣṭhena prathama-sāhasam //*  
*5.63 pāṣāṇena madhyamam //*  
*5.64 śastreṇ uttamam //*  
*5.65 pāda-keśa-aṁśuka-kara-luñcane daśa paṇān //*  
*5.66 śoṇitena vinā duḥkham utpādayitā dvātriṁśat paṇān //*  
*5.67 saha śoṇitena catuḥṣaṣṭim //*  
*5.68 kara-pāda-danta-bhaṅge karṇa-nāsā-vikartane madhyamam //*  
*5.69 ceṣṭā-bhojana-vāg-rodhe prahāra-dāne ca //*  
*5.70 netra-kaṁdharā-bāhu-sakthy-aṁsa-bhaṅge c uttamam //*  
*5.71 ubhaya-netra-bhedinaṁ rājā yāvaj jīvaṁ bandhanān na muñcet\\ //*  
*5.72 tādṛśam eva vā kuryāt\\ //*  
*5.73 ekaṁ bahūnāṁ nighnatāṁ pratyekam uktād daṇḍād dvi-guṇaḥ //*  
*5.74 utkrośantam an-abhidhāvatāṁ tat-samīpa-vartināṁ saṁsaratāṁ ca //*  
*5.75 sarve ca puruṣa-pīḍā-karās tad-utthāna-vyayaṁ dadyuḥ\\ //*  
*5.76 grāmya-paśu-pīḍā-karāś ca //*  
*5.77 go-^aśva-uṣṭra-gaja-apahāry eka-kara-pādaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*5.78 aja-avy-apahāry eka-karaś ca //*  
*5.79 dhānya-apahāry ekādaśa-guṇaṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.80 sasya-apahārī ca //*  
*5.81 suvarṇa-rajata-vastrāṇāṁ pañcāśatas tv abhyadhikam apaharan vikaraḥ //*  
*5.82 tad-ūnam ekādaśa-guṇaṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.83 sūtra-kārpāsa-gomaya-guḍa-dadhi-kṣīra-takra-tṛṇa-lavaṇa-mṛd-bhasma-pakṣi-matsya-*  
*ghṛta-taila-māṁsa-madhu-vaidala-veṇu-mṛnmaya-loha-bhāṇḍānām apahartā mūlyāt tri-*  
*guṇaṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.84 pakva-annānāṁ ca //*  
*5.85 puṣpa-harita-gulma-vallī-latā-parṇānām apaharaṇe pañca-kṛṣṇalam //*  
*5.86 śāka-mūla-phalānāṁ ca //*  
*5.87 ratna-apahāry uttama-sāhasam //*  
*5.88 an-ukta-dravyāṇām apahartā mūlya-samam //*  
*5.89 stenāḥ sarvam apahṛtaṁ dhanikasya dāpyāḥ //*  
*5.90 tatas teṣām abhihita-daṇḍa-prayogaḥ //*  
*5.91 yeṣāṁ deyaḥ panthās teṣām a-patha-dāyī kārṣāpaṇa-pañcaviṁśatiṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.92 āsana-arhasy āsanam a-dadac ca //*  
*5.93 pūjā-arham a-pūjayaṁś ca //*  
*5.94 prātiveśya-brāhmaṇa-nimantraṇa-atikramaṇe ca //*  
*5.95 nimantrayitvā\\ bhojana-a-dāyinaś ca //*  
*5.96 nimantritas tathā^ity uktvā\\ cā-bhuñjānaḥ suvarṇa-māṣakam //*  
*5.97 niketayituś ca dvi-guṇam annam //*  
*5.98 abhakṣyeṇa brāhmaṇasya dūṣayitā ṣoḍaśa suvarṇān //*  
*5.99 jāty-apahāriṇā śatam //*  
*5.100 surayā vadhyaḥ //*  
*5.101 kṣatriyaṁ dūṣayitus tad-ardham //*  
*5.102 vaiśyaṁ dūṣayitus tad-ardham api //*  
*5.103 śūdraṁ dūṣayituḥ prathama-sāhasam //*  
*5.104 a-spṛśyaḥ kāma-kāreṇa spṛśan spṛśyaṁ trai-varṇikaṁ vadhyaḥ //*  
*5.105 rajas-valāṁ śiphābhis tāḍayet\\ //*  
*5.106 pathy-udyāna-udaka-samīpe^apy aśuci-kārī paṇa-śatam //*  
*5.107 tac cāpāsyāt\\ //*  
*5.108 gṛha-bhū-kuḍya-ādy-upabhettā madhyama-sāhasam //*  
*5.109 tac ca yojayet\\ //*  
*5.110 gṛhe pīḍā-karaṁ dravyaṁ prakṣipan paṇa-śatam //*  
*5.111 sādhāraṇa-apalāpī ca //*  
*5.112 preṣitasyā-pradātā ca //*  
*5.113 pitṛ-putra-ācārya-yājya-ṛtvijām anyonya-a-patita-tyāgī ca //*  
*5.114 na ca tān jahyāt\\ //*  
*5.115 śūdra-pravrajitānāṁ daive pitrye bhojakāś ca //*  
*5.116 a-yogya-karma-kārī ca //*  
*5.117 samudra-gṛha-bhedakaś ca //*  
*5.118 a-niyuktaḥ śapatha-kārī //*  
*5.119 paśūnāṁ puṁs-tva-upaghāta-kārī //*  
*5.120 pitā-putra-virodhe sākṣiṇāṁ daśa-paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*5.121 yas tayoś cāntare syāt\\ tasy uttama-sāhasaḥ //*  
*5.122 tulā-māna-kūṭa-kartuś ca //*  
*5.123 tad-a-kūṭe kūṭa-vādinaś ca //*  
*5.124 dravyāṇāṁ pratirūpa-vikrayikasya ca //*  
*5.125 saṁbhūya\\ vaṇijāṁ paṇyam an-argheṇāvarundhatām\\ //*  
*5.126 praty-ekaṁ vikrīṇatāṁ ca //*  
*5.127 gṛhīta-mūlyaṁ yaḥ paṇyaṁ kretur n eva dadyāt\\, tasyāsau sa-udayaṁ dāpyaḥ //*  
*5.128 rājñā ca paṇa-śataṁ daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.129 krītam a-krīṇato yā hāniḥ sā kretur eva syāt\\ //*  
*5.130 rāja-niṣiddhaṁ vikrīṇatas tad-apahāraḥ //*  
*5.131 tarikaḥ sthala-jaṁ śulkaṁ gṛhṇan daśa-paṇān daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.132 brahmacāri-vānaprastha-bhikṣu-gurviṇī-tīrtha-anusāriṇāṁ nāvikaḥ śaulkikaḥ*  
*śulkam ādadānaś ca //*  
*5.133 tac ca teṣāṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*5.134 dyūte kūṭa-akṣa-devināṁ kara-cchedaḥ //*  
*5.135 upadhi-devināṁ saṁdaṁśa-cchedaḥ //*  
*5.136 granthi-bhedakānāṁ ca //*  
*5.137 utkṣepakānāṁ ca kara-cchedaḥ //*  
*5.138 divā paśūnāṁ vṛka-ādy-upaghāte pāle tv an-āyati pāla-doṣaḥ //*  
*5.139 vinaṣṭa-paśu-mūlyaṁ ca svāmine dadyāt\\ //*  
*5.140 an-anujñātāṁ duhan pañcaviṁśatiṁ kārṣāpaṇān //*  
*5.141 mahiṣī cet sasya-nāśaṁ kuryāt\\, tat-pālas tv aṣṭau māṣān daṇḍyaḥ //*  
*5.142 a-pālāyāḥ svāmī //*  
*5.143 aśvas tu^uṣṭro gardabho vā //*  
*5.144 gauś cet tad-ardham //*  
*5.145 tad-ardham aja-avikam //*  
*5.146 bhakṣayitvā\\^upaviṣṭeṣu dvi-guṇam //*  
*5.147 sarvatra svāmine vinaṣṭa-sasya-mūlyaṁ ca //*  
*5.148 pathi grāme vivīta-ante na doṣaḥ //*  
*5.149 an-āvṛte ca //*  
*5.150 alpa-kālam //*  
*5.151 utsṛṣṭa-vṛṣabha-sūtikānāṁ ca //*  
*5.152 yas tu^uttama-varṇān dāsye niyojayet\\ tasy uttama-sāhaso daṇḍaḥ //*  
*5.153 tyakta-pravrajyo rājño dāsyaṁ kuryāt\\ //*  
*5.154 bhṛtakaś cā-pūrṇe kāle bhṛtiṁ tyajan sakalam eva mūlyaṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*5.155 jājñe ca paṇa-śataṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*5.156 tad-doṣeṇa yad vinaśyet\\ tat svāmine //*  
*5.157 anyatra daiva-upaghātāt //*  
*5.158 svāmī cet bhṛtakam apūrṇe kāle jahyāt\\, tasya sarvam eva mūlyaṁ dadyāt\\//*  
*5.159 paṇa-śataṁ ca rājani //*  
*5.160 anyatra bhṛtaka-dośāt //*  
*5.161 yaḥ kanyāṁ pūrva-dattām anyasmai dadyāt\\, sa caura-vac^śāsyaḥ //*  
*5.162 vara-doṣaṁ vinā //*  
*5.163 nir-doṣāṁ parityajan //*  
*5.164 patnīṁ ca //*  
*5.165 a-jānānaḥ prakāśaṁ yaḥ para-dravyaṁ krīṇīyāt\\, tatra tasya na doṣaḥ //*  
*5.166 svāmī dravyam āpnuyāt\\ //*  
*5.167 yady a-prakāśaṁ hīna-mūlyaṁ ca krīṇīyāt\\, tadā kretā vikretā ca caura-vac^śāsyau //*  
*5.168 gaṇa-dravya-apahartā vivāsyaḥ //*  
*5.169 tat-saṁvidaṁ yaś ca laṅghayet\\ //*  
*5.170 nikṣepa-apahāry-artha-vṛddhi-sahitaṁ dhanaṁ dhanikasya dāpyaḥ //*

### 11

*11.01 athāgniḥ //*  
*11.02 ṣoḍaśa-aṅgulaṁ tāvad antaraṁ maṇḍala-saptakaṁ kuryāt\\ //*

### 15

*15.01 atha dvādaśa putrā bhavanti\\ //*  
*15.02 sva-kṣetre saṁskṛtāyām utpāditaḥ svayam aurasaḥ prathamaḥ //*

*15.10 kānīnaḥ pañcamaḥ //*

*15.18 dattakaś cāṣṭamaḥ //*

*15.20 krītaś ca navamaḥ //*  
*15.21 sa ca yena krītaḥ //*  
*15.22 svayam upagato daśamaḥ //*  
*15.23 sa ca yasy upagataḥ //*  
*15.24 apaviddhas tv ekādaśaḥ //*  
*15.25 pitrā mātrā ca parityaktaḥ //*  
*15.26 sa ca yena gṛhītaḥ //*  
*15.27 yatra kva-can utpāditaś ca dvādaśaḥ //*  
*15.28 eteṣāṁ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān //*  
*15.29 sa eva dāya-haraḥ //*  
*15.30 sa cānyān bibhṛyāt\\ //*  
*15.31 an-ūḍhānāṁ sva-vitta-anurūpeṇa saṁskāraṁ kuryāt\\ //*  
*15.32 patita-klība-a-cikitsya-roga-vikalās tv a-bhāga-hāriṇaḥ //*

*15.34 teṣāṁ cāurasāḥ putrā bhāga-hāriṇaḥ //*  
*15.35 na tu patitasya //*  
*15.36 patanīye karmaṇi kṛte tv anantara-utpannāḥ //*  
*15.37 pratilomāsu strīṣu c utpannāś cā-bhāginaḥ //*  
*15.38 tat-putrāḥ paitāmahe^apy arthe //*  
*15.39 aṁśa-grāhibhis te bharaṇīyāḥ //*  
*15.40 yaś cārtha-haraḥ sa piṇḍa-dāyī //*  
*15.41 eka-ūḍhānām apy ekasyāḥ putraḥ sarvāsāṁ putra eva //*  
*15.42 bhrātṛṛṇām eka-jātānāṁ ca //*  
*15.43 putraḥ pitṛ-vitta-a-lābhe^api piṇḍaṁ dadyāt\\ //*

### 18

*18.01 brāhmaṇasya caturṣu varṇeṣu cet putrā bhaveyuḥ\\, te paitṛkaṁ rikthaṁ daśadhā*

*18.04 dvāv aṁśau vaiśyā-putraḥ //*  
*18.05 śūdrā-putras tv ekam //*

*18.13 trīn rājanyaḥ //*  
*18.14 atha brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇa-vaiśyau, tadā ṣaḍdhā vibhaktasya caturo^aṁśān*  
*brāhmaṇas tv ādadyāt\\ //*  
*18.15 dvāv aṁśau vaiśyaḥ //*  
*18.16 atha brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇa-śūdrau putrau syātāṁ\\, tadā tad-dhanam pañcadhā*  
*vibhajeyātām\\ //*  
*18.17 caturo^aṁśān brāhmaṇas tv ādadyāt\\ //*  
*18.18 ekaṁ śūdraḥ //*  
*18.19 atha brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vā kṣatriya-vaiśyau putrau syātāṁ\\, tadā tad-dhanaṁ*  
*pañcadhā vibhajeyātām\\ //*  
*18.20 trīn aṁśān kṣatriyas tv ādadyāt\\ //*  
*18.21 dvāv aṁśau vaiśyaḥ //*  
*18.22 atha brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vā kṣatriya-śūdrau putrau syātāṁ\\, tadā tad-dhanaṁ*  
*caturdhā vibhajeyātām\\ //*  
*18.23 trīn aṁśān kṣatriyas tv ādadyāt\\ //*  
*18.24 ekaṁ śūdraḥ //*

*dhanaṁ tridhā vibhajeyātām\\ //*  
*18.26 dvāv aṁśau vaiśyas tv ādadyāt\\ //*  
*18.27 ekaṁ śūdraḥ //*  
*18.28 ath eka-putrā brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇa-kṣatriya-vaiśyāḥ sarva-harāḥ //*  
*18.29 kṣatriyasya rājanya-vaiśyau //*  
*18.30 vaiśyasya vaiśyaḥ //*  
*18.31 śūdraḥ śūdrasya //*

### 19

*19.01 mṛtaṁ dvijaṁ na śūdreṇa nirhārayet\\ //*  
*19.02 na śūdraṁ dvijena //*  
*19.03 pitaraṁ mātaraṁ ca putrā nirhareyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.04 na dvijaṁ pitaram api śūdrāḥ //*  
*19.05 brāhmaṇam a-nāthaṁ ye brāhmaṇā nirharanti\\ te svarga-loka-bhājaḥ //*  
*19.06 nirhṛtya\\ ca bāndhavaṁ pretaṁ saṁṣkṛtyā-pradakṣiṇena citām abhigamya\\^apsu sa-*  
*vāsaso nimajjanaṁ kuryuḥ\\ //*  
*19.07 pretasy udaka-nirvapaṇaṁ kṛtvā\\^ekaṁ piṇḍaṁ kuśeṣu dadyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.08 privartita-vāsasaś ca nimba-patrāṇi vidaśya\\ dvāry aśmani pada-nyāsaṁ kṛtvā\\*  
*gṛhaṁ praviśeyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.09 akṣatāṁś cāgnau kṣipeyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.10 caturthe divase^asthi-saṁcayanaṁ kuryuḥ\\ //*  
*19.11 teṣāṁ gaṅgā-ambhasi prakṣepaḥ //*

*svarga-lokam adhitiṣṭhati\\ //*  
*19.13 yāvad āśaucaṁ tāvat pretasy udakaṁ piṇḍam ekaṁ ca dadyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.14 krīta-labdha-aśanāś ca bhaveyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.15 a-māṁsa-aśanāś ca //*  
*19.16 sthaṇḍila-śāyinaḥ //*  
*19.17 pṛthak-śāyinaś ca //*  
*19.18 grāmān niṣkramya\\^āśauca-ante kṛta-śmaśru-karmāṇas tila-kalkaiḥ sarṣapa-kalkair*  
*vā snātāḥ parivartita-vāsaso gṛhaṁ praviśeyuḥ\\ //*  
*19.19 tatra śāntiṁ kṛtvā\\ brāhmaṇānāṁ ca pūjanaṁ kuryuḥ\\ //*  
*19.20 devāḥ parokṣa-devāḥ, pratyakṣa-devā brāhmāṇāḥ //*  
*19.21 brāhmaṇair lokā dhāryante\\ //*  
*19.22a/ brāhmaṇānāṁ prasādena divi tiṣṭhanti\\ devatāḥ /*  
*19.22c/ brāhmaṇa-abhihitaṁ vākyaṁ na mithyā jāyate\\ kva-cit //*  
*19.23a/ yad brāhmaṇās tuṣṭa-tamā vadanti\\ tad devatāḥ pratyabhinandayanti\\ /*  
*19.23c/ tuṣṭeṣu tuṣṭāḥ satataṁ bhavanti\\ pratyakṣa-deveṣu parokṣa-devāḥ //*  
*19.24a/ duḥkha-anvitānāṁ mṛta-bāndhavānām āśvāsanaṁ kuryur\\ adīna-sattvāḥ /*  
*19.24c/ vākyais tu yair bhūmi tavābhidhāsye\\ vākyāny ahaṁ tāni mano^abhirāme\\ //*

### 20

*20.01 yad uttara-ayaṇaṁ tad ahar devānām //*  
*20.02 dakṣiṇa-ayanaṁ rātriḥ //*  
*20.03 saṁvatsaro^aho-rātraḥ //*  
*20.04 tat-triṁśatā māsāḥ //*  
*20.05 māsā dvādaśa varṣam //*  
*20.06 dvādaśa varṣa-śatāni divyāni kali-yugam //*  
*20.07 dvi-guṇāni dvāparam //*  
*20.08 tri-guṇāni tretā //*  
*20.09 catur-guṇāni kṛta-yugam //*  
*20.10 dvādaśa-varṣa-sahasrāṇi divyāni catur-yugam //*  
*20.11 catur-yugāṇām eka-saptatir manv-antaram //*  
*20.12 catur-yuga-sahasram ca kalpaḥ //*  
*20.13 sa ca pitāmahasyāhaḥ //*  
*20.14 tāvatī cāsya rātriḥ //*

### 29

*29.01 yas tu^upanīya\\ vrata-ādeśaṁ kṛtvā\\ vedam adhyāpayet\\ tam ācāryaṁ vidyāt\\//*

*29.06 n upanayet\\ //*  
*29.07a/ a-dharmeṇa ca yaḥ prāha\\ yaś cā-dharmeṇa pṛcchati\\ /*

*29.08c/ tatra vidyā na vaktavyā śubhaṁ bījam iv ūṣare //*  
*29.09a/ vidyā ha vai brahmaṇam ājagāma\\ gopāya mā śevadhiṣṭe^aham asti\\ //*  
*29.09c/ a-sūyakāyān-ṛjave^ayatāya na māṁ brūyā\\ a-vīrya-vatī tathā syām\\ //*

### 90

*90.01 mārgaśīrṣa-śukla-pañcadaśyāṁ mṛga-śirasā yuktāyāṁ cūrṇita-lavaṇasya suvarṇa-*  
*nābhaṁ prastham ekaṁ candra-udaye brāhmaṇāya pradāpayet\\ //*  
*90.02 anena karmaṇā rūpa-saubhāgyavān abhijāyate\\ //*  
*90.03 pauṣī cet puṣya-yuktā syāt\\, tasyāṁ gaura-sarṣapa-kalka-udvartita-śarīro gavya-ghṛta-*  
*pūrṇa-kumbhenābhiṣiktaḥ sarva-auṣadhibhiḥ sarva-gandhaiḥ sarva-bījaiś ca snāto ghṛtena*  
*bhagavantaṁ vāsudevaṁ snāpayitvā\\ gandha-puṣpa-dhūpa-dīpa-naivedya-ādibhir abhyarcya\\*  
*vaiṣṇavaiḥ śākrair bārhaspatyaiś ca mantraiḥ pāvake hutvā\\ sa-suvarṇena ghṛtena brāhmaṇān*  
*svasti vācayet\\ //*  
*90.04 vāso-yugaṁ kartre dadyāt\\ //*  
*90.05 anena karmaṇā puṣyate\\ //*  
*90.06 māghī maghā-yutā cet, tasyāṁ tilaiḥ śrāddhaṁ kṛtvā\\ pūto bhavati\\ //*  
*90.07 phālgunī phalgunī-yutā cet, tasyāṁ brāhmaṇāya su-saṁskṛtaṁ svāstīrṇaṁ*  
*śayanaṁ nivedya\\ bhāryāṁ mano-jñāṁ rūpa-vatīṁ draviṇa-vatīṁ c āpnoti\\ //*  
*90.08 nāry api bhartāram //*  
*90.09 caitrī citrā-yutā cet, tasyāṁ citra-vastra-pradānena saubhāgyam āpnoti\\ //*  
*90.10 vaiśākhī viśākhā-yutā cet, tasyāṁ brāhmaṇa-saptakaṁ kṣaudra-yuktais tilaiḥ*  
*saṁtarpya\\ dharma-rājānaṁ prīṇayitvā\\ pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati\\ //*  
*90.11 jyaiṣṭhī jyeṣṭhā-yutā cet syāt\\, tasyāṁ chatra-upānaha-pradānena gava-ādhipatyaṁ*  
*prāpnoti\\ //*  
*90.12 āṣāḍhyām āṣāḍhā-yuktāyām anna-pāna-dānena tad evā-kṣayyam āpnoti\\ //*  
*90.13 śrāvaṇyāṁ śravaṇa-yuktāyāṁ jala-dhenuṁ sa-annāṁ vāso-yugāc chāditāṁ*  
*dattvā\\ svargam āpnoti\\ //*  
*90.14 prauṣṭhapadyāṁ proṣṭhapadā-yuktāyāṁ go-dānena sarva-pāpa-vinirmukto bhavati\\*  
*//*  
*90.15 āśvayujyām aśvinī-gate candramasi ghṛta-pūrṇaṁ bhājanaṁ suvarṇa-yutaṁ viprāya*  
*dattvā\\ dīpta-agnir bhavati\\ //*  
*90.16 kārttikī kṛttikā-yutā cet syāt\\, tasyāṁ sitam ukṣāṇam anya-varṇaṁ vā śaśa-aṅka-*  
*udye sarva-sasya-ratna-gandha-upetaṁ dīpa-madhye brāhmaṇāya dattvā\\ kāntāra-bhayaṁ na*  
*paśyati\\ //*  
*90.17 vaiśākha-śukla-tṛtīyāyām upoṣito^akṣataiḥ śrī-vāsudevam abhyarcya\\ tān eva hutvā*  
*dattvā ca sarva-pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati\\ //*  
*90.18 yac ca tasminn ahani prayacchati\\ tad a-kṣayyatām āpnoti\\ //*  
*90.19 pauṣyāṁ samatītāyāṁ kṛṣṇa-pakṣa-dvādaśyāṁ sa-upavāsas tilaiḥ snātas tila-*  
*ukakaṁ dattvā\\ tilair vāsudevam abhyarcya\\ tān eva hutvā\\ dattvā\\ bhuktvā\\ ca pāpebhyaḥ pūto*  
*bhavati\\ //*  
*90.20 māghyāṁ samatītāyāṁ kṛṣṇa-dvādaśyāṁ sa-upavāsaḥ śravaṇaṁ prāpya\\ śrī-*  
*vāsudeva-agrato mahā-varti-dvayena dīpa-dvayaṁ dadyāt\\ //*  
*90.21 dakṣiṇa-pārśve mahā-rajana-raktena samagreṇa vāsasā ghṛta-tulām aṣṭa-adhikāṁ*  
*dattvā\\ //*  
*90.22 vāma-pārśve tila-taila-yutāṁ sa-aṣṭāṁ dattvā\\ śvetena samagreṇa vāsasā //*  
*90.23 etat kṛtvā\\ yasmin rāṣṭre^abhijāyate\\ yasmin deśe yasmin kule tatr ujjvalo bhavati\\ //*  
*90.24 āśvinaṁ sakalaṁ māsaṁ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pratyahaṁ ghṛtaṁ pradāya\\^aśvinau*  
*prīṇayitvā\\ rūpa-bhāg bhavati\\ //*  
*90.25 tasminn eva māsi pratyahaṁ go-rasair brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā\\ rājya-bhāg bhavati\\ //*  
*90.26 prati-māsaṁ revatī-yute candramasi madhu-ghṛta-yutaṁ pāyasaṁ revatī-prītyai parama-*  
*annaṁ brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā\\ revatīṁ prīṇayitvā\\ rūpasya bhāgī bhavati\\ //*  
*90.27 māghe māsy agniṁ pratyahaṁ tilair hutvā\\ sa-ghṛtaṁ kulmāṣaṁ brāhmaṇān*  
*bhojayitvā\\ dīpta-agnir bhavati\\ //*  
*90.28 sarvāṁ caturdaśīṁ nadī-jale snātvā\\ dharma-rājānaṁ pūjayitvā\\ sarva-pāpebhyaḥ*

### 99

*99.01a/ dṛṣṭvā\\ śriyaṁ deva-devasya viṣṇor gṛhīta-pādāṁ tapasā jvalantīm /*  
*99.01c/ su-tapta-jāmbūnada-cāru-varṇāṁ papraccha\\ devīṁ vasudhā prahṛṣṭā //*  
*99.02a/ unnidra-kokanada-cāru-kare vareṇye unnidra-kokanada-nābhi-gṛhīta-pāde /*  
*99.02c/ unnidra-kokanada-sadma-sadā-sthiti-ite unnidra-kokanada-madhya-samāna-varṇe //*  
*99.03a/ nīla-ab-ja-netre tapanīya-varṇe śukla-ambare ratna-vibhūṣita-aṅgi /*  
*99.03c/ candra-ānane sūrya-samāna-bhāse mahā-prabhāve jagataḥ pradhāne //*  
*99.04a/ tvam eva nidrā jagataḥ pradhānā lakṣmīr dhṛtiḥ śrīr viratir jayā ca /*  
*99.04c/ kāntiḥ prabhā kīrtir atho vibhūtiḥ sarasvatī vāg atha pāvanī ca //*  
*99.05a/ svadhā titikṣā vasudhā pratiṣṭhā sthitiḥ su-dīkṣā ca tathā su-nītiḥ /*  
*99.05c/ khyātir viśālā ca tathā^anasūyā svāhā ca medhā ca tathā^eva buddhiḥ //*  
*99.06a/ ākramya\\ sarvaṁ tu yathā tri-lokīṁ tiṣṭhaty\\ ayaṁ deva-varo^asita-akṣi /*  
*99.06c/ tathā sthitā tvaṁ varade tathā^api pṛcchāmy\\ ahaṁ te vasatiṁ vibhūteḥ //*  
*99.07a/ ity evam uktā vasudhāṁ babhāṣe\\ lakṣmīs tadā deva-vara-agrataḥ-sthā /*  
*99.07c/ sadā sthitā^ahaṁ madhu-sūdanasya devasya pārśve tapanīya-varṇe //*  
*99.08a/ asy ājñayā yaṁ manasā smarāmi\\ śriyā yutaṁ taṁ pravadanti\\ santaḥ /*  
*99.08c/ saṁsmāraṇe cāpy atha yatra cāhaṁ sthitā sadā tac^śṛṇu\\ loka-dhātri //*  
*99.09a/ vasāmy\\ athārke ca niśā-kare ca tārā-gaṇa-āḍhye gagane vimeghe //*  
*99.09c/ meghe tathā lamba-payo-dhare ca śakra-āyudha-āḍhye ca taḍit-prakāśe //*  
*99.10a/ tathā su-varṇe vimale ca rūpye ratneṣu vastreṣv a-maleṣu bhūme /*  
*99.10c/ prāsāda-mālāsu ca pāṇḍurāsu deva-ālayeṣu dhvaja-bhūṣiteṣu //*  
*99.11a/ sadyaḥ kṛte cāpy atha go-maye ca matte gaja-indre turage prahṛṣṭe /*  
*99.11c/ vṛṣe tathā darpa-samanvite ca vipre tathā^evādhyayana-prapanne //*  
*99.12a/ siṁha-āsane c āmalake ca bilve chatre ca śaṅkhe ca tathā^eva padme /*  
*99.12c/ dīpte huta-aśe vimale ca khḍga ādarśa-bimbe ca tathā sthitā^aham //*  
*99.13a/ pūrṇa-uda-kumbheṣu sa-cāmareṣu sa-tāla-vṛnteṣu vibhūṣiteṣu /*  
*99.13c/ bhṛṅgāra-pātreṣu manohareṣu mṛdi sthitā^ahaṁ ca nava-uddhṛtāyām //*  
*99.14a/ kṣīre tathā sarpiṣi śādbale ca kṣaudre tathā dadhni puraṁdhri-gātre /*  
*99.14c/ dehe kumāryāś ca tathā surāṇāṁ tapasvināṁ yajña-hutāṁ ca dehe //*  
*99.15a/ śare ca saṁgrāma-vinirgate ca sthitā mṛte svarga-sadaḥ-prayāte /*  
*99.15c/ veda-dhvanau cāpy atha śaṅkha-śabde svāhā-svadhāyām atha vādya-śabde //*  
*99.16a/ rājya-abhiṣeke ca tathā vivāhe yajñe vare snāta-śirasy athāpi /*  
*99.16c/ puṣpeṣu śukleṣu ca parvateṣu phaleṣu ramyeṣu sarid-varāsu //*  
*99.17a/ saraḥsu pūrṇeṣu tathā jaleṣu sa-śādvalāyāṁ bhuvi padma-khaṇḍe /*  
*99.17c/ vane ca vatse ca śiśau prahṛṣṭe sādhau nare dharma-parāyaṇe ca //*  
*99.18a/ ācāra-seviny atha śāstra-nitye vinīta-veṣe ca tathā su-veṣe //*  
*99.18c/ su-śuddha-dānte mala-varjite ca mṛṣṭa-aśane cātithi-pūjake ca //*  
*99.19a/ svadāra-tuṣṭe nirate ca dharme dharma-utkaṭe cātyaśanād vimukte /*  
*99.19c/ sadā sa-puṣpe sa-su-gandhi-gātre su-gandha-lipte ca vibhūṣite ca //*  
*99.20a/ satye sthite bhūta-hite niviṣṭe kṣama-anvite krodha-vivarjite ca /*  
*99.20c/ sva-kārya-dakṣe para-kārya-dakṣe kalyāṇa-citte ca sadā vinīte //*  
*99.21a/ nārīṣu nityaṁ su-vibhūṣitāsu pati-vratāsu priya-vādinīṣu /*  
*99.21c/ a-mukta-hastāsu suta-anvitāsu su-gupta-bhāṇḍāsu bali-priyāsu //*  
*99.22a/ saṁmṛṣṭa-veśmāsu jita-indriyāsu kali-vyapetāsv avilolupāsu /*  
*99.22c/ dharma-vyapekṣāsu dayā-anvitāsu sthitā sadā^ahaṁ madhu-sūdane ca //*  
*99.23a/ nimeṣa-mātraṁ ca vinā kṛtā^ahaṁ na jātu tiṣṭhe\\ puruṣa-uttamena // (ā half verse.)*

### 100
